● Update(ALGORITHM.md)
  ⎿  Added 377 lines
      1053  | `--first-state-timeout` | 30.0 s | abort if no leader state
             arrives within this window |
      1054  | `--dry-run`       | off      | connect + read + compute + l
            og, but no arm/takeoff/offboard |
      1055  | `--land-on-exit`  | off      | issue `land()` on every dron
            e on shutdown |
      1056 +                                                             
      1057 +---                                                          
      1058 +                                                             
      1059 +## 14. End-to-end walkthrough — PX4 SITL + MAVSDK + QGroundCo
           +ntrol                                                        
      1060 +                                                             
      1061 +The full path from "I have PX4 and QGC installed" to "four dr
           +ones flying                                                  
      1062 +a spear formation visible in QGC". Each step lists the comman
           +d, what to                                                   
      1063 +check, and what goes wrong.                                  
      1064 +                                                             
      1065 +### 14.1 Prerequisites (one-time)                            
      1066 +                                                             
      1067 +You need:                                                    
      1068 +                                                             
      1069 +- **PX4 built** at a known path. In this walkthrough we use  
      1070 +  `$HOME/PX4-Autopilot` — adjust to wherever yours is.       
      1071 +  ```bash                                                    
      1072 +  cd $HOME/PX4-Autopilot                                     
      1073 +  make px4_sitl_default                                      
      1074 +  ```                                                        
      1075 +  Takes ~15–20 minutes first time. Rebuild needed only after 
           +pulling new                                                  
      1076 +  PX4 sources.                                               
      1077 +                                                             
      1078 +- **mavsdk_server binary** somewhere on disk. Download Linux 
           +x64 from                                                     
      1079 +  https://github.com/mavlink/MAVSDK/releases (pick a release 
           +matching                                                     
      1080 +  the pip `mavsdk` package version). In this repo it's alread
           +y at                                                         
      1081 +  `/home/senthilkumarl/mavsdk_drone_show/mavsdk_server`.     
      1082 +                                                             
      1083 +- **MAVSDK Python package**:                                 
      1084 +  ```bash                                                    
      1085 +  pip install mavsdk                                         
      1086 +  ```                                                        
      1087 +                                                             
      1088 +- **QGroundControl** installed (AppImage or packaged). Alread
           +y set up if                                                  
      1089 +  you've ever flown PX4 SITL on this machine.                
      1090 +                                                             
      1091 +- **Python deps for Simple_swarm**: `numpy pandas matplotlib`
           +.                                                            
      1092 +                                                             
      1093 +### 14.2 Step 1 — Verify the PX4 build                       
      1094 +                                                             
      1095 +```bash                                                      
      1096 +# PX4 binary                                                 
      1097 +ls $HOME/PX4-Autopilot/build/px4_sitl_default/bin/px4        
      1098 +                                                             
      1099 +# SIH airframe — this is the one we want                     
      1100 +ls $HOME/PX4-Autopilot/build/px4_sitl_default/etc/init.d-posi
           +x/airframes \                                                
      1101 +   | grep sihsim                                             
      1102 +```                                                          
      1103 +Expect to see `10040_sihsim_quadx`. If missing, rebuild.     
      1104 +                                                             
      1105 +> **Why SIH (Simulator-In-Hardware) and not `none_iris` or Ga
           +zebo?**                                                      
      1106 +> - `none_iris` launches PX4 but requires an external simulat
           +or on TCP                                                    
      1107 +>   4560+N. Without one, `simulator_mavlink` blocks, GPS neve
           +r populates,                                                 
      1108 +>   arm always fails with "Preflight Fail: GPS not ok".      
      1109 +> - `Tools/simulation/gazebo-classic/sitl_multiple_run.sh` do
           +esn't work                                                   
      1110 +>   on Gazebo Classic 11 — the script calls `gz model --spawn
           +-file=…`                                                     
      1111 +>   which is Ignition syntax; models never spawn, every PX4 w
           +aits                                                         
      1112 +>   forever.                                                 
      1113 +> - `sihsim_quadx` bundles PX4's internal SIH physics loop.  
      1114 +>   `SENS_EN_GPSSIM / BAROSIM / MAGSIM` are all set in the ai
           +rframe, so                                                   
      1115 +>   sensors populate, EKF converges, and drones are actually 
           +arm-able.                                                    
      1116 +>   Headless, no external dependencies.                      
      1117 +                                                             
      1118 +### 14.3 Step 2 — Prepare scenario files                     
      1119 +                                                             
      1120 +Under `Simple_swarm/test_inputs/<your_scenario>/` create thre
           +e files:                                                     
      1121 +                                                             
      1122 +**`drones_config.json`** — hw_id → MAVSDK UDP port mapping (`
           +14540+hw_id-1`):                                             
      1123 +```json                                                      
      1124 +{                                                            
      1125 +  "drones": [                                                
      1126 +    {"drone_id": 1, "hw_id": 1, "ip": "udp://:14540", "port":
           + 14540, "is_leader": true},                                  
      1127 +    {"drone_id": 2, "hw_id": 2, "ip": "udp://:14541", "port":
           + 14541, "is_leader": false},                                 
      1128 +    {"drone_id": 3, "hw_id": 3, "ip": "udp://:14542", "port":
           + 14542, "is_leader": false},                                 
      1129 +    {"drone_id": 4, "hw_id": 4, "ip": "udp://:14543", "port":
           + 14543, "is_leader": false}                                  
      1130 +  ]                                                          
      1131 +}                                                            
      1132 +```                                                          
      1133 +                                                             
      1134 +**`formation.csv`** — rigid body-frame offsets relative to th
           +e leader                                                     
      1135 +(see §4.2 for conventions):                                  
      1136 +```                                                          
      1137 +hw_id,follow,offset_n,offset_e,offset_alt                    
      1138 +1,0,0,0,0                                                    
      1139 +2,1,-3,-3,0                                                  
      1140 +3,1,-5,0,0                                                   
      1141 +4,1,-3,3,0                                                   
      1142 +```                                                          
      1143 +                                                             
      1144 +**`leader_path.csv`** — NED coords + heading over wall-clock 
           +time. See                                                    
      1145 +§4.3 for column conventions; optional `heading` is degrees wi
           +th 0°=N.                                                     
      1146 +For a 40 m-radius circle over 45 s:                          
      1147 +```python                                                    
      1148 +import csv, math                                             
      1149 +rows = [["t","north","east","altitude","heading"]]           
      1150 +R, PERIOD, HOVER, ALT = 20.0, 40.0, 5.0, 15.0                
      1151 +t = 0.0                                                      
      1152 +while t <= HOVER + PERIOD + 1e-6:                            
      1153 +    alpha = 0.0 if t <= HOVER else 2*math.pi*(t-HOVER)/PERIOD
      1154 +    n = R*math.cos(alpha); e = R*math.sin(alpha)             
      1155 +    hdg = 90.0 if t <= HOVER else 90.0 + 360.0*(t-HOVER)/PERI
           +OD                                                           
      1156 +    rows.append([round(t,3), round(n,3), round(e,3), ALT, rou
           +nd(hdg,3)])                                                  
      1157 +    t += 0.5                                                 
      1158 +with open("leader_path.csv","w",newline="") as f: csv.writer(
           +f).writerows(rows)                                           
      1159 +```                                                          
      1160 +                                                             
      1161 +### 14.4 Step 3 — Launch N PX4 SITL instances (headless, SIH)
      1162 +                                                             
      1163 +Clean any residue, then launch one PX4 process per drone. Eac
           +h instance                                                   
      1164 +needs its own working directory (for `dataman`, params, logs)
           + and a                                                       
      1165 +unique `-i N`:                                               
      1166 +                                                             
      1167 +```bash                                                      
      1168 +pkill -9 -f 'bin/px4 -i' 2>/dev/null; sleep 2                
      1169 +                                                             
      1170 +PX4_BIN=$HOME/PX4-Autopilot/build/px4_sitl_default/bin/px4   
      1171 +PX4_ETC=$HOME/PX4-Autopilot/build/px4_sitl_default/etc       
      1172 +                                                             
      1173 +for i in 0 1 2 3; do                                         
      1174 +  rm -rf /tmp/sitl_run/rootfs_$i                             
      1175 +  mkdir -p /tmp/sitl_run/rootfs_$i                           
      1176 +  lon=$(python3 -c "print(77.0695 + $i * 0.0005)")           
      1177 +  nohup bash -c "cd /tmp/sitl_run/rootfs_$i && exec env \    
      1178 +    PX4_SIM_MODEL=sihsim_quadx \                             
      1179 +    PX4_HOME_LAT=28.4523 PX4_HOME_LON=$lon PX4_HOME_ALT=200 \
      1180 +    HEADLESS=1 \                                             
      1181 +    $PX4_BIN -i $i -d $PX4_ETC" \                            
      1182 +    > /tmp/sitl_run/px4_sih_$i.log 2>&1 &                    
      1183 +  sleep 3   # stagger startup so mavlink instances don't coll
           +ide                                                          
      1184 +done                                                         
      1185 +```                                                          
      1186 +                                                             
      1187 +Important:                                                   
      1188 +- `-i N` = instance number. Drone hw_id = N+1. SysID = N+1 (a
           +uto-assigned).                                               
      1189 +- `-d <path>` = absolute path to the built `etc/` dir.       
      1190 +- `cd /tmp/sitl_run/rootfs_$i` BEFORE launch — PX4 writes `da
           +taman`,                                                      
      1191 +  `parameters.bson`, `log/` in CWD. Don't share rootfs betwee
           +n instances.                                                 
      1192 +- `PX4_HOME_LAT / LON / ALT` = simulated spawn position. Stag
           +ger LON so                                                   
      1193 +  the drones spawn ~50 m apart (required for SITL — can't sha
           +re XY).                                                      
      1194 +- Use `nohup bash -c "... exec env …"` — without `exec env`, 
           +the                                                          
      1195 +  `env VAR=val` becomes a subshell command that may not propa
           +gate.                                                        
      1196 +  Without `nohup`, the processes die when your terminal close
           +s.                                                           
      1197 +                                                             
      1198 +Verify:                                                      
      1199 +```bash                                                      
      1200 +pgrep -af 'bin/px4 -i'                                       
      1201 +# expect 4 processes (one per instance)                      
      1202 +                                                             
      1203 +ss -ulnp 2>/dev/null | grep -E '1857[0-3]|1458[0-3]|1454[0-3]
           +'                                                            
      1204 +# expect PX4 listening on 18570+N (GCS local), 14580+N (MAVSD
           +K local),                                                    
      1205 +# and mavsdk_server on 14540+N once it's running (next step) 
      1206 +```                                                          
      1207 +                                                             
      1208 +### 14.5 Step 4 — Launch N mavsdk_server processes           
      1209 +                                                             
      1210 +One gRPC bridge per drone, pairing a gRPC server port with th
           +e drone's                                                    
      1211 +MAVLink UDP port:                                            
      1212 +                                                             
      1213 +```bash                                                      
      1214 +MAVSDK=/path/to/mavsdk_server    # adjust                    
      1215 +for i in 0 1 2 3; do                                         
      1216 +  $MAVSDK -p $((50041+i)) udp://:$((14540+i)) \              
      1217 +    > /tmp/sitl_run/mavsdk_$i.log 2>&1 &                     
      1218 +done                                                         
      1219 +```                                                          
      1220 +                                                             
      1221 +Verify:                                                      
      1222 +```bash                                                      
      1223 +ss -lnp 2>/dev/null | grep mavsdk_server                     
      1224 +# expect 4 UDP listeners on 14540..14543 and 4 TCP listeners 
           +on 50041..50044                                              
      1225 +```                                                          
      1226 +                                                             
      1227 +Port mapping, authoritative source `px4-rc.mavlink` (§7 in th
           +is doc):                                                     
      1228 +- drone N (instance i=N-1): gRPC `50040+N`, MAVLink UDP `1454
           +0+i` =                                                       
      1229 +  `14540+N-1`.                                               
      1230 +- `drones_config.json` assumes this mapping — don't change po
           +rt                                                           
      1231 +  conventions without updating the config.                   
      1232 +                                                             
      1233 +### 14.6 Step 5 — Wait for EKF heading convergence           
      1234 +                                                             
      1235 +PX4's EKF takes **5–15 seconds** after startup to stabilize h
           +eading                                                       
      1236 +(during which `is_global_position_ok` returns false and arm w
           +ill                                                          
      1237 +reject). Wait until every instance has emitted "Ready for tak
           +eoff":                                                       
      1238 +                                                             
      1239 +```bash                                                      
      1240 +# poll every 2s until all 4 say Ready                        
      1241 +until all_ready=true; for i in 0 1 2 3; do                   
      1242 +  grep -q 'Ready for takeoff' /tmp/sitl_run/px4_sih_$i.log ||
           + all_ready=false                                             
      1243 +done; $all_ready; do sleep 2; done                           
      1244 +```                                                          
      1245 +                                                             
      1246 +Or just wait 15 seconds after the last PX4 launched — usually
           + enough.                                                     
      1247 +                                                             
      1248 +### 14.7 Step 6 — Set up QGroundControl                      
      1249 +                                                             
      1250 +Open QGC. By default it has a UDP Auto-Connect link listening
           + on                                                          
      1251 +`:14550`. PX4 sends GCS heartbeats to that port; QGC should a
           +uto-discover                                                 
      1252 +all drones within a few seconds.                             
      1253 +                                                             
      1254 +**In QGC verify:**                                           
      1255 +                                                             
      1256 +1. Click the vehicle selector at the top-left. Dropdown shoul
           +d show 4                                                     
      1257 +   entries (SysID 1, 2, 3, 4). If only some appear:          
      1258 +   - `Application Settings → General → "UDP Auto-Connect"` = 
           +ON.                                                          
      1259 +   - If running PX4 without broadcast (default), restart QGC 
           +*after*                                                      
      1260 +     PX4 — QGC's heartbeats then reach PX4's GCS ports and ea
           +ch drone                                                     
      1261 +     replies.                                                
      1262 +2. Map view is centered on the drones. They spawn at `lat 28.
           +4523°N`                                                      
      1263 +   (or whatever `PX4_HOME_LAT` you set). Click any vehicle in
           + the                                                         
      1264 +   selector — QGC should snap to it.                         
      1265 +3. Add a Values Panel at the bottom. Add Altitude, Horizontal
           + Velocity,                                                   
      1266 +   Yaw. These tick live during flight independent of map view
           +.                                                            
      1267 +                                                             
      1268 +**If still invisible:**                                      
      1269 +                                                             
      1270 +- Add explicit UDP comm links to each PX4 GCS local port     
      1271 +  (`Application Settings → Comm Links → Add → UDP → Port 1857
           +0/18571/…`).                                                 
      1272 +  Less elegant than auto-discover but bulletproof.           
      1273 +- Check that QGC is actually listening: `ss -ulnp | grep QGro
           +und` should                                                  
      1274 +  show `:14550`.                                             
      1275 +                                                             
      1276 +### 14.8 Step 7 — Dry-run the controller                     
      1277 +                                                             
      1278 +Before arming anything, confirm plumbing end-to-end with `--d
           +ry-run`.                                                     
      1279 +It connects, reads telemetry, computes setpoints, writes logs
           + — but                                                       
      1280 +does NOT arm/takeoff/offboard.                               
      1281 +                                                             
      1282 +```bash                                                      
      1283 +cd Simple_swarm                                              
      1284 +python3 realtime_swarm_mavsdk.py \                           
      1285 +  --config        test_inputs/<your_scenario>/drones_config.j
           +son \                                                        
      1286 +  --formation     test_inputs/<your_scenario>/formation.csv \
      1287 +  --leader-source csv \                                      
      1288 +  --leader-path   test_inputs/<your_scenario>/leader_path.csv
           + \                                                           
      1289 +  --duration 10 --dry-run                                    
      1290 +```                                                          
      1291 +                                                             
      1292 +Expected output:                                             
      1293 +```                                                          
      1294 +[main] leader=1  followers=[2, 3, 4]                         
      1295 +[hw_id 1] connecting on mavsdk_server :50041 ...             
      1296 +[hw_id 1] mavsdk connected                                   
      1297 +[hw_id 2] connecting on mavsdk_server :50042 ...             
      1298 +[hw_id 2] mavsdk connected                                   
      1299 +... (same for 3, 4)                                          
      1300 +[ctrl] will also push CSV setpoints to the leader drone (hw_i
           +d 1)                                                         
      1301 +[ctrl] running at 20.0 Hz for 10.0s                          
      1302 +[ctrl] control loop done — 200 ticks                         
      1303 +[main] logs in .../output/mavsdk_realtime/                   
      1304 +```                                                          
      1305 +                                                             
      1306 +Check `output/mavsdk_realtime/drone_*_commanded.csv` — these 
           +contain                                                      
      1307 +what the controller *would* have pushed. If the numbers look 
           +sensible                                                     
      1308 +(leader position tracing the CSV, followers offset by the rot
           +ation),                                                      
      1309 +the math is right.                                           
      1310 +                                                             
      1311 +### 14.9 Step 8 — Fly the swarm                              
      1312 +                                                             
      1313 +Remove `--dry-run`, add `--takeoff-alt` and `--land-on-exit`:
      1314 +                                                             
      1315 +```bash                                                      
      1316 +python3 realtime_swarm_mavsdk.py \                           
      1317 +  --config        test_inputs/<your_scenario>/drones_config.j
           +son \                                                        
      1318 +  --formation     test_inputs/<your_scenario>/formation.csv \
      1319 +  --leader-source csv \                                      
      1320 +  --leader-path   test_inputs/<your_scenario>/leader_path.csv
           + \                                                           
      1321 +  --takeoff-alt 15 --control-rate 20 --duration 132 --land-on
           +-exit                                                        
      1322 +```                                                          
      1323 +                                                             
      1324 +You should see:                                              
      1325 +```                                                          
      1326 +[hw_id 1] GPS ok                                             
      1327 +[hw_id 2] GPS ok                                             
      1328 +...                                                          
      1329 +[hw_id 1] offboard takeoff → 15.0 m                          
      1330 +[hw_id 2] offboard takeoff → 15.0 m                          
      1331 +...                                                          
      1332 +[ctrl] will also push CSV setpoints to the leader drone (hw_i
           +d 1)                                                         
      1333 +[ctrl] running at 20.0 Hz for 132.0s                         
      1334 +[ctrl] control loop done — 2640 ticks                        
      1335 +[main] logs in .../output/mavsdk_realtime/                   
      1336 +```                                                          
      1337 +                                                             
      1338 +In QGC: **all 4 vehicles** should move — leader tracing the p
           +lanned                                                       
      1339 +path, followers maintaining spear formation around it. Watch 
           +the                                                          
      1340 +altitude / velocity values in the Values Panel to confirm mot
           +ion.                                                         
      1341 +                                                             
      1342 +> **Why all 4 move (and not just the followers):**           
      1343 +> With `--leader-source csv`, the controller does two things 
      1344 +> simultaneously:                                            
      1345 +> 1. Subscribes to the real leader drone's MAVSDK telemetry →
      1346 +>    populates `LeaderState` → drives **follower** setpoints 
           +so they                                                      
      1347 +>    formation-lock onto where the leader actually is.       
      1348 +> 2. Interpolates the CSV path at current wall-clock time → d
           +rives the                                                    
      1349 +>    **leader** drone along the planned path via offboard set
           +points.                                                      
      1350 +>                                                            
      1351 +> This means followers track the real leader (not the idealiz
           +ed CSV),                                                     
      1352 +> which eliminates the "formation offset from real leader" is
           +sue that                                                     
      1353 +> occurs if you compute follower targets off the CSV directly
           +.                                                            
      1354 +                                                             
      1355 +### 14.10 Step 9 — Validate the formation                    
      1356 +                                                             
      1357 +```bash                                                      
      1358 +python3 validate_formation.py \                              
      1359 +  --trajectories-dir output/mavsdk_realtime \                
      1360 +  --config      test_inputs/<your_scenario>/drones_config.jso
           +n \                                                          
      1361 +  --formation   test_inputs/<your_scenario>/formation.csv \  
      1362 +  --start-after 20.0 --tol-pos 2.5 --tol-heading 10.0        
      1363 +```                                                          
      1364 +                                                             
      1365 +Checks two invariants:                                       
      1366 +                                                             
      1367 +1. **Formation position**: at every sample (after takeoff tra
           +nsient)                                                      
      1368 +   each follower sat within `tol-pos` m of                   
      1369 +   `leader_position + R(leader_heading) · offset_body`.      
      1370 +2. **Tip points to heading**: the vector from the tail (deepe
           +st-aft                                                       
      1371 +   follower) to the leader aligns with the leader's heading w
           +ithin                                                        
      1372 +   `tol-heading` degrees.                                    
      1373 +                                                             
      1374 +Expected numbers for real SITL with PX4 SIH at 20 Hz control:
      1375 +- Peak position error during steady-state: 1–2 m             
      1376 +- RMS position: 0.5–1 m                                      
      1377 +- Peak heading alignment during continuous rotation: 5–15°   
      1378 +- Peak errors spike 2–4 m during the first few seconds after 
           +takeoff                                                      
      1379 +  (PD lag while quads accelerate to the rotating formation)  
      1380 +                                                             
      1381 +### 14.11 Step 10 — Visualize                                
      1382 +                                                             
      1383 +```bash                                                      
      1384 +python3 visualize_trajectories.py \                          
      1385 +  --trajectories-dir output/mavsdk_realtime \                
      1386 +  --save-dir output/mavsdk_realtime --no-show                
      1387 +                                                             
      1388 +python3 animate_trajectories.py \                            
      1389 +  --trajectories-dir output/mavsdk_realtime \                
      1390 +  --output output/mavsdk_realtime/achieved.gif               
      1391 +```                                                          
      1392 +                                                             
      1393 +Outputs:                                                     
      1394 +- `swarm_trajectories.png` — 4-panel static plot: 3D paths, t
           +op-down,                                                     
      1395 +  altitude vs time, formation-in-leader-translated-frame (bes
           +t shows                                                      
      1396 +  rotation).                                                 
      1397 +- `achieved.gif` — 3D animation with fading trail + heading a
           +rrows.                                                       
      1398 +                                                             
      1399 +### 14.12 Step 11 — Cleanup                                  
      1400 +                                                             
      1401 +```bash                                                      
      1402 +for p in $(pgrep -f 'bin/px4 -i'); do kill $p; done          
      1403 +pkill -x mavsdk_server                                       
      1404 +# Close QGC from the UI, or pkill QGroundControl             
      1405 +```                                                          
      1406 +                                                             
      1407 +### 14.13 Troubleshooting (real failures seen in the wild)   
      1408 +                                                             
      1409 +| Symptom | Cause | Fix |                                    
      1410 +|---|---|---|                                                
      1411 +| `TimeoutError: hw_id N: no mavsdk_server on 127.0.0.1:5004N
           +` | `mavsdk_server` not running on that port | Check `ss -lnp
           + \| grep mavsdk_server`. Restart missing one. |              
      1412 +| `TimeoutError: hw_id N GPS not ok within 30.0s` | EKF not c
           +onverged yet, or PX4 using `none_iris` (no sim sensors) | Wai
           +t longer, or switch to `sihsim_quadx`. |                     
      1413 +| `OffboardError: NO_SETPOINT_SET` | `offboard.start()` calle
           +d without a prior `set_position_ned()` | Confirmed `arm_and_o
           +ffboard_takeoff` seeds a position setpoint before `start()`. 
           +|                                                            
      1414 +| `Preflight Fail: heading estimate not stable` (in PX4 log) 
           +| EKF hasn't finished initial convergence | Wait ~15 s after 
           +PX4 starts. Normal during boot. |                            
      1415 +| QGC shows 0 vehicles | QGC restarted after PX4; drones latc
           +hed onto old QGC partner IP | Restart PX4 instances, then sta
           +rt QGC. Or add explicit UDP comm links in QGC for each PX4 GC
           +S port (`18570+N`). |                                        
      1416 +| QGC shows vehicle 1 but it never moves | `--leader-source c
           +sv` without the leader-drives path (old versions); or arming 
           +silently failed on drone 1 | Verify `realtime_swarm_mavsdk.py
           +` line that says `will also push CSV setpoints to the leader 
           +drone (hw_id 1)`. That's the drive-leader log. If missing, up
           +date the controller. |                                       
      1417 +| "Waypoints out of sequence" warning in QGC | `drone.action.
           +takeoff()` creates a stranded takeoff mission item; QGC's mis
           +sion sync complains with 4 drones racing | Use `arm_and_offbo
           +ard_takeoff()` (does NOT call `action.takeoff()`); it seeds o
           +ffboard and arms directly. |                                 
      1418 +| PX4 exits immediately with "Exiting NOW" | Stale process ho
           +lding port, or rootfs locked by another instance | `pkill -9 
           +-f 'bin/px4'` + `rm -rf /tmp/sitl_run/rootfs_*` + relaunch. |
      1419 +| `sitl_multiple_run.sh` spawns nothing | Gazebo Classic 11 d
           +oesn't support `gz model --spawn-file=…` | Don't use that scr
           +ipt. Use the `sihsim_quadx` loop in Step 14.4. |             
      1420 +| `cannot join current thread` / `_MultiThreadedRendezvous CA
           +NCELLED` at Python exit | mavsdk/aiogrpc destructor cleanup d
           +uring interpreter shutdown | Harmless. The flight already com
           +pleted by the time you see it. |                             
      1421 +| Tracking error much worse than expected | `--leader-source 
           +csv` without the leader being flown; followers chase idealize
           +d leader while real drone 1 sits on ground | Confirm the cont
           +roller version includes `drive_leader = True` path, AND the l
           +eader drone appears as armed+in-air in QGC. |                
      1422 +                                                             
      1423 +### 14.14 Expected end-state — what "working" looks like     
      1424 +                                                             
      1425 +After running the full walkthrough you should have:          
      1426 +                                                             
      1427 +- **4 PX4 instances** running headless with SIH physics (`pgr
           +ep px4` → 4).                                                
      1428 +- **4 mavsdk_server instances** bridging gRPC ↔ MAVLink (`pgr
           +ep mavsdk_server` → 4).                                      
      1429 +- **QGC** showing 4 vehicles in the selector, all reporting a
           +rmed → in-air → landed states in sync.                       
      1430 +- **`output/mavsdk_realtime/`** containing per-drone CSVs (`d
           +rone_<N>.csv` achieved, `drone_<N>_commanded.csv` what was se
           +nt) plus `swarm_trajectories.png`.                           
      1431 +- **`validate_formation.py`** reporting PASS on both position
           + (≤2.5 m) and heading alignment (≤10°) checks.               
      1432 +- A **4-panel plot** where the bottom-right "formation in lea
           +der-translated frame" shows all 8 snapshot polygons nearly ov
           +erlapping — the invariant proof that the spear tip always poi
           +nts along leader heading.

  Summary of the update to Simple_swarm/ALGORITHM.md

  §14. End-to-end walkthrough — PX4 SITL + MAVSDK + QGroundControl — new section
   with 14 subsections:

  Step: Prereqs

  What it covers: PX4 build, mavsdk_server binary, MAVSDK Python, QGC, Python
    deps
  ────────────────────────────────────────

  What it covers: Check bin/px4 exists, confirm 10040_sihsim_quadx airframe is
    built. Includes a callout explaining why SIH, not none_iris (no sim sensors
  →
     arm fails), not Gazebo multi-SITL (broken on Classic 11)
  ────────────────────────────────────────

    (with a Python snippet for a circular path)

  What it covers: Shell loop that spawns N PX4 instances with nohup,
  per-instance
    rootfs, staggered homes. Explicit warnings about cd before launch, exec env,

    nohup
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Step: 4
  Subsection: 14.5 Launch mavsdk_server
  What it covers: One bridge per drone; port convention table
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Step: 5

  expectation

    + followers track real leader telemetry" architecture so the user knows why
    all 4 drones move

  What it covers: validate_formation.py usage; expected numbers for real SITL

  What it covers: Table of every real failure we hit this session: mavsdk_server

    missing, GPS timeout, NO_SETPOINT_SET, preflight heading, QGC empty, vehicle

    1 idle, "waypoints out of sequence", PX4 exit, Gazebo multi-sitl broken,
    cannot join current thread, poor tracking from idealized-leader math
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Step: —
  Subsection: 14.14 Success check
  What it covers: Definition of "working" — 4 PX4, 4 mavsdk, 4 vehicles in QGC,
    logs produced, validator PASS, formation polygon snapshots nearly overlap

  Also updated §12.3 to cross-reference §14 and to flag that multiple_sitl.sh is
   known-broken on this system.

  Key things captured that were painful discoveries during the session

  1. Use sihsim_quadx as the airframe — not the popular none_iris. That single
  insight would have saved an hour.
  2. cd $rootfs && exec env $VARS $PX4_BIN is the correct launch incantation —
  missing exec env drops environment, missing cd mixes rootfs between instances.
  3. PX4 port map is in px4-rc.mavlink — documented authoritatively rather than
  guessed.
  4. QGC auto-discovery relies on mutual heartbeat handshake — when QGC restarts
   mid-flight, drones keep talking to the old (dead) QGC unless you restart the
  drones. Workaround: start order matters.
  5. --leader-source csv requires leader drone to be flown too — otherwise QGC's
   vehicle 1 is a stationary icon. The controller now does both (drive leader
  from CSV, compute followers from leader's telemetry).
  6. Don't call action.takeoff() — it creates a mission waypoint that QGC
  complains about. The arm_and_offboard_takeoff() helper seeds offboard and arms
   directly.
  7. Gazebo Classic multi-SITL is dead on this system — gz model --spawn-file=…
  is Ignition-only. The walkthrough tells the user to skip it.

  The document is now a durable artifact: anyone (future Claude, future
  collaborator, future you) can follow §14 start-to-finish and get 4 drones
  flying a formation in QGC without re-discovering each of the landmines.

✻ Churned for 3m 34s
